###############################
# Mainline rite types         #
###############################
# One rite per faith that would otherwise only ever have an engine-generated
# dynamic rite. Dynamic rites get positional tags (dynamic_rite_%i) that script,
# history and localisation cannot reference, so every faith gets a stable
# scripted main rite instead.
# Keys, and therefore names, adjectives and adherent locs, match the parent
# faith so the player-facing text is unchanged.
# color, tenets, doctrines and tenet_selection_pair are copied from the faith
# type: an empty tenets block on a rite falls back to the religion's core
# tenets rather than the faith's, which would silently drop faith tenets.
#{

nyame = { # Nyame — the religion of the Akan and Guan: Nyame, the creator, who is approached through the abosom; Asase Yaa, the earth; Tano and the gods of the rivers; Anansi the spider; the nsamanfo, the ancestors; the akomfo, who dance and are possessed; the sacred grove at Tanoboase, from which the Bono came. Main rite
	name = nyame
	desc = nyame_desc
	faith = akom_pagan

	color = { 0.9 0.7 0.3 }
	icon = akanism

	tenets = {
		tenet_adorcism # Akom, the dance in which the obosom takes hold of the okomfo and speaks through him
		tenet_ritual_celebrations # The yam festival, which honours the dead, gives thanks for the harvest and cleanses the town
		tenet_ancestor_worship # The nsamanfo, the ancestors, who are fed and consulted by their lineage

		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_women_shunned

		doctrine_deviancy_shunned

		mz_doctrine_no_syncretism
		unreformed_faith_doctrine
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted

	}

		FertilityGodName = nyame_fertility_god_name

perkunas = { # Perkūnas — the religion of the Lithuanians, Samogitians and Curonians: Perkūnas the thunder and Dievas the sky, Žemyna the earth, Laima who allots each fate, Gabija of the hearth; the alka groves and hills; the sacred fire kept at Vilnius; the dead burned with their horses and arms. Main rite
	name = perkunas
	desc = perkunas_desc
	faith = baltic_pagan

	tenets = {
		tenet_sacred_childbirth # Laima, who stands by every birth and allots the child its fate

bimo = { # Bimoism — the religion of the Yi (Nuosu) of Liangshan, the Wumeng hills and the Dian lakes, heirs of the Wuman of Nanzhao: Ngeti Gunzy, god of the sky; Zhyge Alu, who shot down the suns; the bimo, hereditary priests who read and curse from books in the Yi script and send the souls of the dead on to the ancestors; the suni, drummers possessed by the spirits; the dead burned on the pyre. Main rite
	name = bimo
	desc = bimo_desc
	faith = bimoism

	color = { 154 123 93 }
	icon = bimoism

	tenets = {
		tenet_esotericism # The bimo's books and curses, learned only within the bimo lineages
		tenet_ancestor_worship # The ancestors' souls, kept in the maddu and sent on to the land of the ancestors by the bimo
		tenet_adorcism # The suni, drumming in trance as the spirits possess them
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_male_dominated
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic
		doctrine_theocracy_temporal
		doctrine_concubines # Lords kept secondary wives; marriage stayed within one's own caste
		doctrine_divorce_allowed
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_cousins # Cross-cousin marriage was preferred; marriage within the clan was forbidden
		doctrine_clerical_function_recruitment # The bimo cursed the enemy and read the omens before a raid
		doctrine_clerical_gender_male_only # Bimo were men, the office passing from father to son
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_crime
		doctrine_adultery_women_crime
		doctrine_kinslaying_extended_family_crime # The clan avenges its own; feuds ran for generations
		doctrine_deviancy_crime
		doctrine_witchcraft_shunned # Families suspected of sorcery were shunned, and their daughters could not marry out
		doctrine_pilgrimage_local_rites
		doctrine_funeral_cremation # The dead were burned, as the Wuman of Nanzhao had burned theirs
		doctrine_monasticism_absent
		doctrine_sacraments_absent
		doctrine_no_anointment
		doctrine_mythic_literary # The bimo scriptures, written in the Yi script
		mz_doctrine_eastern_syncretism
		unreformed_faith_doctrine
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted # Liquor drunk from lacquered cups at every gathering
		doctrine_me_ablution_none
	}

	localization = {

		HighGodHerHis = CHARACTER_HERHIS_HIS
		HighGodNameAlternate = yi_bimo_high_god_name_alternate
		HighGodNameAlternatePossessive = yi_bimo_high_god_name_alternate_possessive

		CreatorName = yi_bimo_creator_god_name
		CreatorNamePossessive = yi_bimo_creator_god_name_possessive
		CreatorSheHe = CHARACTER_SHEHE_HE
		CreatorHerHis = CHARACTER_HERHIS_HIS
		CreatorHerHim = CHARACTER_HERHIM_HIM

		HealthGodName = yi_bimo_health_god_name
		HealthGodNamePossessive = yi_bimo_health_god_name_possessive
		HealthGodSheHe = CHARACTER_SHEHE_HE
		HealthGodHerHis = CHARACTER_HERHIS_HIS
		HealthGodHerHim = CHARACTER_HERHIM_HIM

		FertilityGodName = yi_bimo_fertility_god_name
		FertilityGodNamePossessive = yi_bimo_fertility_god_name_possessive

		FertilityGodHerHis = CHARACTER_HERHIS_HIS

		WealthGodName = yi_bimo_wealth_god_name
		WealthGodNamePossessive = yi_bimo_wealth_god_name_possessive

		WealthGodHerHim = CHARACTER_HERHIM_HIM

		HouseholdGodNamePossessive = yi_bimo_household_god_name_possessive
		HouseholdGodSheHe = CHARACTER_SHEHE_IT

dayaw = { # Dayawism — the Visayan religion of the diwata and the umalagad ancestor spirits; Kaptan and Magwayen, Laon of Kanlaon, Sidapa on Madya-as; the babaylan and asog mediums. Beliefs recorded by Miguel de Loarca (1582) and Francisco Alcina (1668). Main rite
	name = dayaw
	desc = dayaw_desc
	faith = dayawism

	color = { 125 229 84 }
	icon = dayawism

	tenets = {
		tenet_ancestor_worship
		tenet_mountain_worship
		tenet_adorcism
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_equal # Daughters inherited equally with sons; women kept their own property and bride-price (Plasencia, Loarca)
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic
		doctrine_theocracy_temporal # The datu rules; the babaylan serves
		doctrine_concubines # One principal wife; datus kept secondary wives and slave women
		doctrine_divorce_allowed # Divorce by return of the bride-price
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_cousins
		doctrine_homosexuality_accepted # The asog, men who lived as women and served as babaylan
		doctrine_adultery_men_shunned # Settled by fines
		doctrine_adultery_women_shunned
		doctrine_kinslaying_close_kin_crime
		doctrine_deviancy_shunned
		doctrine_witchcraft_shunned # Sorcerers (mambabarang, aswang) were feared and killed
		doctrine_clerical_function_alms_and_pacification # The babaylan healed the sick and kept the rites of the dead
		doctrine_clerical_gender_female_only # Babaylan were women, or asog who lived as women
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment # Called by the diwata through illness or dream
		doctrine_pilgrimage_local_rites # Offerings at the village's own sacred places; no journeys to distant shrines
		doctrine_funeral_bewailment # Kin wailed over the dead and kept the mourning taboos (Alcina)
		doctrine_monasticism_absent
		doctrine_sacraments_absent

		doctrine_oral_storytelling

hyang = { # Kejawèn — the hyang and ancestor worship of the Javanese countryside beneath the Hindu-Buddhist courts: the danyang village spirits and punden terraces, the sacred mountains, the petirtaan bathing places, and the hermits (wiku, ajar) of the mountain mandalas described in the Tantu Panggelaran. Main rite
	name = hyang
	desc = hyang_desc
	faith = javanist

	color = { 0.43 0.02 0.09 }
	icon = kejawen

	tenets = {
		tenet_ancestor_worship # The danyang, founding spirits of each village, and the punden terraces of the ancestors
		tenet_mountain_worship # Dieng ("Di Hyang"), Lawu, Merapi, and Mahameru, which the gods carried from India to steady Java (Tantu Panggelaran)
		tenet_inner_journey # Tapa: the hermit's fasting and meditation in caves and on mountain tops
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_male_dominated
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic
		doctrine_theocracy_lay_clergy # The wiku and ajar of the mountain hermitages stand outside the court
		doctrine_polygamy
		doctrine_divorce_allowed
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_cousins
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_crime # Paradara, adultery, is among the gravest offences of the Kutara Manawa law code
		doctrine_adultery_women_crime
		doctrine_kinslaying_close_kin_crime
		doctrine_deviancy_crime
		doctrine_witchcraft_shunned # Calon Arang, the witch-widow of Girah, brought plague on Airlangga's kingdom
		doctrine_clerical_function_alms_and_pacification
		doctrine_clerical_gender_male_only
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment
		mz_doctrine_eastern_syncretism # The hyang beneath the Hindu-Buddhist gods: Bhatara Guru and Dewi Sri are Javanese as much as Indic
		unreformed_faith_doctrine
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted # Tuak palm wine and brem
		doctrine_me_ablution_full # Petirtaan: the sacred bathing places (Jalatunda, Belahan)
	}

pulugaism = { # Pūlugaism — the religion of the Ongan peoples, the Onge of Little Andaman and the Jarawa, who kept apart from all outsiders: Pūluga, the maker of the world who sent the flood, and his sons the winds. Main rite
	faith = pulugaism

	color = { 51 191 156 }

	tenets = {
		tenet_ancestor_worship # The bones of the dead are kept and worn
		tenet_sanctity_of_nature # Pūluga punishes the waste of food and the breaking of taboos with storms
		tenet_communal_identity # The Ongan peoples kept apart from every outsider
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_equal
		doctrine_pluralism_righteous # Strangers landing on their shores were driven off
		doctrine_theocracy_temporal
		doctrine_monogamy # A couple who have had a child stay together for life
		doctrine_divorce_approval
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_restricted
		doctrine_clerical_function_alms_and_pacification
		doctrine_clerical_gender_male_only # The dreamers who speak with the spirits are men
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_shunned

bilikuism = { # Bilikuism — the religion of the Great Andamanese: Biliku, who sends the northeast monsoon and the storms that punish broken taboos, and Tarai of the southwest monsoon; the spirits of the dead and the bones worn as relics; the oko-jumu, the dreamers. Recorded by E. H. Man (1883) and A. R. Radcliffe-Brown (1906-08). Main rite

		tenet_ancestor_worship # The bones of the dead are worn as relics that heal and protect

dab_qhuas = { # Kev Dab Qhuas — the religion of the Hmong (Miao) of the Guizhou, western Hunan and southern Sichuan hills: the ancestors of each clan and the household spirits of the xwm kab altar; Siv Yis, the first shaman, and the txiv neeb who follow him into the spirit world to bring back the lost soul; the qeej and the Qhuab Ke, which show the dead the road back to the ancestors; Ntxwj Nyug, lord of the land of the dead. Main rite
	faith = dab_qhuas

	color = { 115 79 150 }
	icon = dab_qhuas

	tenets = {
		tenet_ancestor_worship # The ancestors of each clan, fed at the New Year and at every sacrifice
		tenet_adorcism # The txiv neeb in trance on the shaman's bench, riding with the helping spirits into the spirit world
		tenet_reincarnation # The soul returns to the ancestors and is born again
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_no_head
		doctrine_gender_male_dominated # Patrilineal clans; a bride joins her husband's clan and his ancestors
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic
		doctrine_theocracy_temporal
		doctrine_polygamy # A man of standing kept several wives, and took his dead brother's widow as his own
		doctrine_divorce_approval # Settled by the elders of both clans, with the bride-price returned
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_cousins # Marriage within the clan was forbidden; a cousin of another clan was the preferred match
		doctrine_clerical_function_alms_and_pacification # The txiv neeb heal by bringing back the lost soul
		doctrine_clerical_gender_either # Txiv neeb are men and women alike
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_allowed
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment # A txiv neeb is chosen by the spirits through sickness
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_women_crime
		doctrine_kinslaying_extended_family_crime # The clan avenges its own
		doctrine_deviancy_shunned
		doctrine_witchcraft_shunned
		doctrine_pilgrimage_local_rites
		doctrine_funeral_bewailment # Days of mourning with the qeej and the drum, while the Qhuab Ke shows the soul the road back to the ancestors
		doctrine_monasticism_absent
		doctrine_sacraments_absent
		doctrine_no_anointment
		doctrine_oral_storytelling # No writing; the history of the clans was kept in songs and funeral chants

		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted # Rice liquor at every sacrifice

rrmea = { # Rrmea — the religion of the Qiang: Mubyasei, god of the sky, whose emblem is the white stone set on every roof; the mountain gods, the gods of the house, the hearth and the fields; the xbi, who wear the monkey-skin hat and beat the sheepskin drum, heal the sick and drive out Dul; the sheep sacrificed to the mountain at the New Year; the dead burned on the pyre. 	faith = rrmeaism

	color = { 0.75 0.74 0.84 }

	}

uchinaa = { # Okinawan — the religion of Okinawa and Amami: Amamichu, who came down from heaven and made the islands and their sacred groves; Nirai Kanai, the land beyond the sea from which the gods come each year and to which the dead return; the utaki, groves where the gods descend and no man may enter; the noro, the women who serve the gods for each village, and the yuta who speak for the dead; the spiritual power of a sister over her brother; Hinukan, the god of the hearth. 	name = uchinaa
	desc = uchinaa_desc

		tenet_ancestor_worship # The ancestors in the family tomb, honoured by the whole lineage
		tenet_adorcism # The yuta, women possessed by the gods, who speak for the dead

singbonga = { # Singbonga — the religion of the Munda, Ho and Bhumij of Chota Nagpur: Singbonga, the sun, and Chando, the moon; the sarna grove of every village and its pahan; the burial stones of the clans; Sarhul at the blossoming of the sal. Main rite

	desc = singbonga_desc

		tenet_sanctity_of_nature # The sarna, the grove of sal trees left standing when the village was cleared, where the gods of the land live

mihriyya = { # Mihriyya — the followers of the archegos Mihr, who under the Umayyads loosened the rules governing dealings between Manichaeans and outsiders, the wisallat; the larger party of the church of Babylon. Main rite
	name = mihriyya
	desc = mihriyya_desc
	faith = manichean_babylon

	color = { 50 168 82 }
	icon = wizrobed_manichean

	tenets = {
		tenet_gnosticism # The two principles of Light and Darkness, and the light held captive in matter that the knowledge given by Mani sets free
		tenet_asceticism # The seals of the mouth, the hand and the breast that bound the Elect
		tenet_adaptive # Mihr loosened the rules on dealings with outsiders, so that Manichaeans could serve and accept gifts from the officials of the caliph
	}

	doctrines = {
		doctrine_spiritual_head # The archegos, successor of Mani, whose seat was Babylon
		doctrine_gender_male_dominated
		doctrine_pluralism_pluralistic # Mani held that Zoroaster, the Buddha and Jesus had all been true apostles before him
		doctrine_theocracy_temporal
		doctrine_monogamy # A Hearer was to be content with one wife
		doctrine_divorce_approval
		doctrine_bastardry_legitimization
		doctrine_consanguinity_restricted
		doctrine_clerical_function_alms_and_pacification # The Hearers fed the Elect, and the Elect prayed for the Hearers and freed the light in their food
		doctrine_clerical_gender_male_only # The teachers, bishops and presbyters were men; women could be Elect, but not of the hierarchy
		doctrine_clerical_marriage_disallowed # The Elect were bound to chastity
		doctrine_clerical_succession_spiritual_appointment # The archegos named the teachers and bishops
		doctrine_homosexuality_shunned
		doctrine_adultery_men_crime # Among the ten commandments of the Hearers
		doctrine_adultery_women_crime
		doctrine_kinslaying_any_dynasty_member_crime # The Elect might not kill even an animal, and the Hearers might not kill a man
		doctrine_deviancy_shunned
		doctrine_witchcraft_crime # Sorcery was forbidden to the Hearers
		doctrine_pilgrimage_forbidden # No place of pilgrimage; the Bema was kept in every community before the empty seat of Mani
		doctrine_funeral_stoic # The body is a prison of darkness; the soul alone ascends
		doctrine_monasticism_accepted
		doctrine_sacraments_absent # No baptism and no eucharist; the sacred meal of the Elect and the confession of the Hearers took their place
		doctrine_no_anointment
		doctrine_scriptural # The Living Gospel, the Treasure of Life, the Pragmateia and the other books written by Mani himself
		mz_doctrine_christian_syncretism # Mani called himself the apostle of Jesus Christ and the Paraclete he had promised
		doctrine_me_intoxication_shunned # The Elect drank no wine
		doctrine_me_ablution_none # Mani forbade the washings of the Baptists among whom he grew up, for water too holds the light
	}

	doctrines = {
		special_doctrine_is_western_christian_faith
		doctrine_me_intoxication_accepted
		doctrine_me_ablution_handwashing
	}
}

julianists = {
	faith = julianists

	color = { 141 78 12 }

		requires_dlc_flag = by_god_alone
		tenet = tenet_miaphysitism # A non-Chalcedonian party of the 6th century
		fallback_tenet = tenet_asceticism
	}
	tenet_selection_pair = {

	}
